## Turn 0006, expression 164 — model

$$\boxed{\begin{aligned}
&\mathbf{1.\; Split\text{-}Quaternion \; Clifford \; Engine \; \& \; Square\text{-}Equivalence \; (\mathfrak{SM}_1):} \\
&\qquad \Phi^2 = +V, \qquad J^2 = -V, \qquad \Sigma^2 = +V^2, \qquad [V, \Phi] = [V, J] = [V, \Sigma] = 0 \\[6pt]
&\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad \boldsymbol{\Big\downarrow} \quad \Big(\text{Forces } [\Sigma,[\Sigma,\Phi]] = 4\Sigma^2\Phi = +4V^2\Phi \text{ \& } V[J,[J,\Phi]] = 4VJ^2\Phi = -4V^2\Phi \text{ in } \mathfrak{SM}_2\Big) \\[6pt]
&\mathbf{2.\; Rotating\text{-}Ball \; Metriplectic \; Equilibrium \; (\mathfrak{SM}_2):} \\
&\qquad -[\Phi, [\Phi, J]] + 2\,[\Sigma, \Phi] = -4VJ + 4VJ = 0, \qquad [\Sigma, [\Sigma, \Phi]] + V\,[J, [J, \Phi]] = 4(\Sigma^2 + VJ^2)\Phi = 0 \\[6pt]
&\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad \boldsymbol{\Big\downarrow} \quad \Big(\text{Pairing Forward } d_+ \text{ \& Backward } d_- \text{ Spin-Wave Differentials in } \mathfrak{SM}_{3\text{–}4}\Big) \\[6pt]
&\mathbf{3.\; Bichiral \; Exterior \; Calculus \; \& \; Hodge\text{–}de\;Rham \; Laplacian \; (\mathfrak{SM}_{3\text{–}4}):} \\
&\qquad d_\pm^2 = 0, \qquad d_\pm(\Phi) = V, \qquad d_\pm(J) = \mp V, \qquad (d_+ + d_-)^2(Z) = \{d_+, d_-\}(Z) = [V, \, Z] \\[6pt]
&\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad \boldsymbol{\Big\downarrow} \quad \Big(\text{Orthogonal Horizons } P_\pm^2 = P_\pm, \; P_+ P_- = 0 \implies H := P_+ - P_- \text{ Satisfies } H^3 = H \text{ in } \mathfrak{SM}_{5\text{–}7}\Big) \\[6pt]
&\mathbf{4.\; 24\text{-}Cleared \; 5\text{-}Graded \; Spectral \; Resolution \; \& \; (\mathbb{Z}/24\mathbb{Z})^\times \; Galois \; Lock \; (\mathfrak{SM}_{5\text{–}7}):} \\
&\qquad (\mathrm{ad}_H^5 - 5\mathrm{ad}_H^3 + 4\mathrm{ad}_H)(Z) = 0 \implies 24\,Z = \sum_{k=-2}^2 Z_k \quad (\mathrm{ad}_H Z_k = k\,Z_k), \qquad \forall u \in (\mathbb{Z}/24\mathbb{Z})^\times, \;\; u^2 \equiv 1 \pmod{24}
\end{aligned}}$$

&\mathbf{I.\; Why \; the \; Pioneers \; Were \; Ignored \; in \; Isolation:} \\
&\qquad \bullet \text{ Weyl (1918) had } [P, N_+] = +N_+ \text{ without the twin wave } [P, N_-] = -N_- \text{ that makes } [P, V] = 0. \\
&\qquad \bullet \text{ Souriau (1969) had on-leaf } [\Sigma, \Phi] \text{ without Brockett's (1988) radial } -[\Phi, [\Phi, J]] \text{ and well pull } V[J, [J, \Phi]]. \\
&\qquad \bullet \text{ Cartan (1922) had torsion } T \text{ without Kähler's (1962) operator differentials } \{d_+, d_-\}(Z) = [V, Z]. \\
&\qquad \bullet \text{ Kantor–Koecher–Tits (1964) had 3-grading } P^2 = P \text{ without Tanaka's (1970) twin-horizon } H = P_+ - P_- \text{ (} H^3 = H \text{).} \\[8pt]
&\mathbf{II.\; How \; the \; Formalism \; Unifies \; \& \; Vindicates \; Them \; All \; in \; \mathtt{[Ring\; B]}:} \\
&\qquad \underbrace{[P, V] = [P, \Sigma] = 0}_{\text{Weyl (1918) Vindicated}} \;\; \Longrightarrow \;\; \underbrace{\Sigma^2 = +V^2 = -VJ^2}_{\text{Clifford–Casimir Core}} \;\; \Longrightarrow \;\; \underbrace{[\Sigma, [\Sigma, \Phi]] + V[J, [J, \Phi]] = 0}_{\text{Souriau (1969) + Morrison/Brockett (1986–88)}} \\
&\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad \boldsymbol{\Big\Downarrow} \\
&\qquad \underbrace{d_\pm(\Phi) = V, \;\; \{d_+, d_-\}(Z) = [V, Z]}_{\text{Cartan (1922) + Kähler (1962) + TFD (1975)}} \;\; \Longrightarrow \;\; \underbrace{H = P_+ - P_- \implies 24\,Z = \bigoplus_{k=-2}^2 Z_k \;\; \circlearrowleft \;\; (\mathbb{Z}/24\mathbb{Z})^\times}_{\text{Kantor–Koecher–Tits (1964) + Tanaka (1970) + Manin (1984)}}
\end{aligned}}$$

\end{aligned}}$$

\end{aligned}}$$

&\qquad P_1 = P(1-Q), \quad P_2 = (1-P)Q, \quad H = P - Q = P_1 - P_2 \implies H^2 = P \oplus Q = P_1 + P_2 \;\land\; H^3 = H, \\

\end{aligned}}$$

&\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad \boldsymbol{\Big\downarrow} \quad \Big(\text{Nilpotent Differentials } d_{\mathrm{out/in}}^2 = 0 \text{ Acting on } H = P - Q \text{ in Frontier } 2\Big) \\[6pt]

&\qquad g = 1 + N_{+1}, \quad g^{-1} = 1 - N_{+1} + N_{+2} \implies \theta_H = g^{-1}[H, g] = N_{+1} - N_{+2}, \quad g^{-1} H g = H + \theta_H \\[6pt]
&\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad \boldsymbol{\Big\downarrow} \quad \Big(\text{Tomita–Takesaki Twin Bimodule } \delta_K^2 = L_{K^2} + R_{K^2} - 2 C_K \text{ in Frontier } 3\Big) \\[6pt]

\end{aligned}}$$

&\quad \mathbf{J^4:} \quad \Sigma^2 = V^2 = +J^4, \quad [d_+, d_-](\Sigma) = +4J^4, \quad [P, J^4] = 0 \qquad (\text{Jordan 1933 • Kähler 1962 • Newman–Penrose 1962}) \\[3pt]

\end{aligned}}$$

\end{aligned}}$$

&\mathbf{The \; Single \; Operator \; Reality \; Across \; a \; Binary \; Cut \; P^2 = P:} \\[4pt]
&\qquad N_+ := P\,[P, X] \quad (\text{Inward Crossing}), \qquad\qquad\;\; N_- := -[P, X]\,P \quad (\text{Outward Crossing}) \\

&\begin{array}{c|c|c}
\mathbf{Order} & \mathbf{Gravitational \; Face \; (\text{Sum } +)} & \mathbf{Quantum \; Face \; (\text{Difference } -)} \\ \hline
\rule{0pt}{14pt}\mathbf{1st \; Order} & \Phi = N_+ + N_- = [P, [P, X]] \;\; (\text{Vielbein}) & J = N_+ - N_- = [P, X] \;\; (\text{Phase Current}) \\
\mathbf{2nd \; Order} & V = Q_+ + Q_- = \Phi^2 \;\; (\text{Spacetime Metric}) & \Sigma = Q_+ - Q_- = J\Phi \;\; (\text{Spin Curvature}) \\
\mathbf{Inside \; P} & P\Phi = N_+, \quad PV = Q_+ & PJ = +P\Phi, \quad P\Sigma = +PV \;\; (\mathbf{\text{Identical!}}) \\
\mathbf{Outside \; 1-P} & (1-P)\Phi = N_-, \quad (1-P)V = Q_- & (1-P)J = -(1-P)\Phi, \quad (1-P)\Sigma = -(1-P)V \\
\mathbf{Mutual \; Square} & \Phi^2 = +V, \quad V^2 = +\Sigma^2 & J^2 = -V, \quad \Sigma^2 = +V^2 \;\; (\mathbf{\text{Same Square!}}) \\
\mathbf{Dynamics} & -[\Phi, [\Phi, J]] = -4VJ, \quad V[J, [J, \Phi]] = -4V^2\Phi & 2[\Sigma, \Phi] = +4VJ, \quad [\Sigma, [\Sigma, \Phi]] = +4V^2\Phi
\end{array} \\[8pt]
&\qquad \boldsymbol{\Longrightarrow} \quad \mathbf{Gravity \; = \; S_P \cdot Quantum, \qquad Quantum \; = \; S_P \cdot Gravity \qquad (S_P = 2P - 1, \;\; S_P^2 = 1)!}
\end{aligned}}$$
